Conclusion: 所提出的MI-TMPC框架能够有效处理非凸城市空中走廊的无人机运动规划问题，兼具可行性保证与全局收敛能力，可简化传统分层规划架构。

Abstract: This work presents a motion planning framework for UAV navigation in non-convex urban air corridors. The planner is based on a mixed-integer tracking model predictive control formulation that enforces corridor feasibility and dynamic consistency within a single optimization problem. To guarantee convergence to the target and mitigate the occurrence of local minima induced by non-convex geometry, a shortest-path-based offset cost with feasibility constraints is embedded directly into the planning problem. Numerical simulations show that the proposed formulation generates dynamically valid trajectories that satisfy the corridor constraints and converge to the target without relying on external global planning stages.

</details>


### [177] [ArmnetBench v0.1: Parallel Real-World Evaluation of Manipulation Policies on a Low-Cost Arm Farm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24481)
*Praveen Selvaraj,Lorenzo Uttini,Ville Kuosmanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArmnetBench v0.1是一个基于低成本SO-101机械臂集群的机器人操作策略评估基准，包含2,518次策略 rollout和600次参考演示，共3,118个带三分类标签（成功/次优/失败）的轨迹，支持单臂和双臂配置下的7种策略在12个任务上的对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界评估是开发通用机器人操作策略的瓶颈，因为每次 rollout都需要物理硬件和操作员进行设置、重置和评分，效率低下且成本高昂。

Method: 构建低成本SO-101机械臂集群（arm farm），在轻度现场监督下自动化运行；每个任务提供50个演示进行训练/微调；人类对策略 rollout进行三分类评分（成功/次优/失败）；以LeRobot v3.0和RoboMeter格式发布核心数据集。

Result: 完成2,518次策略 rollout和600次参考演示，共计3,118个带质量标签的轨迹；在12个任务上对比了7种策略（含单臂和双臂配置）；建立了初始排行榜。

Conclusion: 该基准验证了低成本机械臂集群在轻度监督下进行端到端策略评估的可行性；带质量标签的轨迹数据可支持下游学习任务（如奖励建模、预测世界模型、混合质量数据训练策略等）。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation is a bottleneck in developing generalist robot manipulation policies. Each rollout requires physical hardware and an operator to set up, reset, and score it. We introduce ArmnetBench v0.1, a benchmark run on a fleet of low-cost SO-101 cells under light on-site supervision. v0.1 validates this arm farm end to end and compares 7 policies across 12 tasks with both single-arm and bimanual configurations. Each policy is trained or fine-tuned on 50 demonstrations per task; the benchmark contains 2,518 policy rollouts and 600 reference demonstrations. All 3,118 episodes carry a three-way label (successful, suboptimal, or failure). Policy rollouts are human-scored, while demonstrations are successful by construction. Beyond evaluation, its quality-labelled trajectories support downstream learning, from reward and predictive world models to policies trained on mixed-quality data. The leaderboard is an initial comparison under this shared budget. We release the 3,118 core episodes in LeRobot v3.0 and RoboMeter formats.

</details>


### [178] [Distributed Coordination for Resilient Multi-UAV Remote Sensing: A Photovoltaic Inspection Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24482)
*Guillermo GP-Lenza,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Martin Molina,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SwarmLink——一种非侵入式的无人机群对等通信基础设施，无需修改主机系统即可为现有飞行框架添加协同能力。将其应用于光伏电站巡检场景，通过分布式拍卖机制统一处理初始目标分配、平台故障恢复和数据质量触发的重分配，三种中断场景均归约为同一重拍卖流程，无需地面站介入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机遥感虽能按比例缩短任务时间，但实际收益依赖机群的运行时协调（目标分配、故障响应、数据质量恢复）。传统集中式地面站协调存在单点故障风险：链路中断或站故障会导致感知盲区，需操作员介入才能填补。

Method: 提出SwarmLink，一种对等通信基础设施，非侵入式地为现有飞行框架（如Aerostack2）添加协同能力。设计分布式拍卖机制，将初始目标分配、平台故障恢复和数据质量触发的重分配统一为同一运行时机制，无需修改核心系统，也无需地面站参与。

Result: 三种中断场景（初始分配、平台故障、数据质量问题）均归约为同一重拍卖流程，在Aerostack2上实现零核心修改的运行时间协调。

Conclusion: SwarmLink通过非侵入式对等通信架构消除了集中式地面站的单点故障，为大规模无人机巡检任务提供了去中心化的弹性协调方案，在光伏电站巡检场景中得到验证。

Abstract: Deploying multiple UAVs for remote sensing enables proportional reductions in mission time, but realizing these benefits requires the fleet to coordinate at runtime: distributing sensing targets, responding to platform failures, and recovering from degraded data quality. In inspection campaigns, where mission value depends on complete coverage and the usability of every capture, a centralized ground-station coordinator is a single point of failure: a lost link or station fault leaves sensing gaps that cannot be filled without operator intervention. We propose the \textbf{SwarmLink}, an inter-agent communication infrastructure that non-invasively extends any existing aerial framework with peer-to-peer coordination capability, without modifying the host system. We apply it to photovoltaic plant inspection as a representative large-scale sensing campaign, extending Aerostack2 with a distributed auction that unifies initial sensing-target allocation, platform-failure recovery, and data-quality-triggered reassignment into a single runtime mechanism. All three disruption scenarios reduce to the same re-auction over remaining targets and active platforms, requiring zero modifications to the Aerostack2 core and no ground-station involvement during the mission.

</details>


### [179] [τ: Learning Touch-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Models from Future Visual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24485)
*Ning Cheng,Jinan Xu,Wanlin Li,Yangzhi Chen,Jing Gao,Yiqun Wang,Kelan Peng,Wenjuan Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出τ框架，通过JEPA启发的未来视觉监督学习动作条件化时空触觉表征，并融合视觉语言特征进行动作生成，在有限数据下提升操作性能；同时引入TacAura数据集，实验证明τ在接触丰富操作任务中优于现有模型并具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将触觉表征适配到预训练VLA模型时面临数据和建模层面的双重挑战：数据层面，任务特定演示有限限制表征质量，大规模预训练成本高；建模层面，现有方法要么关注瞬时接触状态，要么用6D力/力矩序列建模时间交互动态，忽视了高维触觉信号。

Method: 提出τ框架，采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）思想，从未来视觉监督中学习动作条件化的时空触觉表征，在潜在空间中运行且仅在训练时使用；然后将触觉表征与视觉语言特征融合用于动作生成。

Result: τ优于现有模型，能泛化到未见过的物体和场景，提升了操作性能和鲁棒性。同时发布了包含同步视觉、本体感知和基于视觉的触觉信号的TacAura数据集。

Conclusion: τ框架成功解决了有限数据下触觉表征学习与VLA模型适配的挑战，通过未来视觉监督的JEPA方法实现了高效的时空触觉表征学习，为接触丰富的机器人操作任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning the informative tactile representation while effectively adapting it to pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains challenging at both the data and modeling levels. At the data level, limited task-specific demonstrations constrain representation quality, whereas large-scale pretraining incurs substantial costs. At the modeling level, existing methods either focus on instantaneous contact states or model temporal interaction dynamics using 6D wrench sequences, leaving high-dimensional tactile signals underexplored. To address these challenges, we present τ, a touch-augmented VLA framework that learns an action-conditioned spatiotemporal tactile representation from future visual supervision inspired by the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), and fuses it with vision-language features for action generation under limited data. This supervision operates in latent space and is used only during training, adding no deployment overhead. We also introduce TacAura, a dataset of synchronized vision, proprioception, and vision-based tactile signals across four representative contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experiments show that τ outperforms existing models and generalizes to unseen objects and scenes, delivering improved manipulation performance and robustness

</details>


### [180] [KAI: A Kinematic-Aware Interface for Data-Efficient Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24493)
*Yaping Li,Zhaxizhuoma,Qiaojun Yu,Jia Zeng,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出运动学感知的关节接口（KAI），一种捕捉关节物体运动学结构的结构化中间表征。通过将可解释的几何与运动学先验嵌入策略学习，KAI在仅使用一半演示数据的情况下，在六个仿真任务中达到82.9%的平均成功率，并展现出对未见背景和视觉干扰物的鲁棒泛化能力，其动作无关设计还可通过人类交互视频协同训练进一步提升真实世界鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关节物体操作需要理解运动学结构，但仅靠机器人演示学习这一结构困难且成本高昂。

Method: 提出KAI（Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface），一种结构化中间表征，将可解释的几何与运动学先验嵌入策略学习，提供与关节运动底层结构对齐的强归纳偏置。

Result: 在六个仿真任务中以一半演示数据达到82.9%平均成功率，匹配或超越基线性能；泛化到未见背景和视觉干扰物；通过人类交互视频协同训练，在多样化视觉干扰下达到超70%平均成功率。

Conclusion: KAI通过结构化中间表征有效提升了样本效率和泛化能力，其动作无关设计为利用人类视频数据提升真实世界鲁棒性提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation requires an understanding of kinematic structure that is difficult and costly to learn from robot demonstrations alone. We introduce the Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface (KAI), a structured intermediate representation that captures the kinematic structure of articulated objects. By embedding interpretable geometric and kinematic priors into policy learning, KAI provides a strong inductive bias aligned with the underlying structure of articulated motion. This design effectively improves sample efficiency, with gains particularly pronounced in low-data regimes: across six simulation tasks, our method achieves an average success rate of 82.9%, matching or surpassing baseline performance while using only half the demonstration data. Our method also exhibits robust generalization to unseen backgrounds and visual distractors, transferring from a single clean training environment to cluttered real-world scenes. KAI's action-agnostic design further enables co-training with human interaction videos to enhance real-world robustness: under diverse visual distractions, our method with video co-training achieves over 70% average success rate.

</details>


### [181] [NEO: NeRF It Once, Edit It Many Times for Continuous Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24538)
*Mikołaj Zieliński,David Hall,Dominik Belter,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NEO，一个统一框架，用于机器人操作的语言引导NeRF编辑，包括语言引导的对象移除、基于知识蒸馏的直接NeRF权重编辑方法，以及首个用于评估机器人操作场景编辑的基准数据集NEO-Dataset。实验表明在对象移除和抓取放置任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NeRF场景编辑方法在机器人操作场景中存在视觉伪影、几何不一致等问题，缺乏专门针对机器人操作任务的NeRF编辑评估基准，无法有效支持机器人对未来场景状态的建模与预演。

Method: (i) 语言引导对象移除：结合神经场重采样与多视图一致的渐进式修补；(ii) 直接NeRF权重编辑：利用知识蒸馏，通过教师-学生模型组合原始NeRF和编辑后NeRF，实现机器人执行动作前对未来场景状态的一致建模；(iii) 构建NEO-Dataset基准数据集。

Result: 在对象移除和抓取放置机器人实验中，NEO在场景编辑任务上优于现有最先进基线方法，生成视觉一致且几何一致的编辑结果，减少了先前方法常见的伪影。

Conclusion: NEO框架通过语言引导的NeRF编辑有效支持机器人操作任务，在视觉质量和几何一致性方面均优于现有方法，为机器人场景编辑提供了统一的解决方案和评估基准。

Abstract: In this paper, we present NEO, a unified framework providing language-guided NeRF editing for robotic manipulation. Our paper introduces (i) a language-guided object removal that combines neural field resampling with multiview-consistent progressive inpainting, (ii) a direct NeRF weight editing method utilizing knowledge distillation, composing original and edited NeRFs via a teacher-student model, enabling coherent modeling of future scene states before a robot executes an action, and (iii) the first benchmark (NEO-Dataset) for quantitatively evaluating NeRF scene editing methods suitable for robot manipulation. We show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in scene editing tasks, including object removal and pick-and-place robotic experiments, yielding visually coherent and geometrically consistent edits that reduce artifacts commonly introduced by prior methods.

</details>


### [182] [Development of a Handheld Actuation Mechanism for a Tendon-driven Robotically Steered Guidewire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24629)
*Saima Chavenet,Timothy A. Brumfiel,Revanth Konda,Jaydev P. Desai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种手持式驱动机构，用于肌腱驱动的机器人导丝，通过紧凑的绕线机制实现电机化导丝转向以及手动旋转和平移，并在解剖学准确的主动脉模型中进行导航验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手动导丝导航因血管迂曲和尖端缺乏可转向性而具有挑战性，可能导致血管损伤、穿孔、夹层、闭塞以及术后并发症（如血栓形成），因此需要更安全、可控的导丝操控方案。

Method: 利用紧凑绕线机制设计手持式驱动装置，集成电机驱动的肌腱张力控制实现导丝弯曲转向，同时保留手动旋转和进退自由度，通过手柄上的摇杆和瞬时开关控制最长1.5米的可转向导丝。

Result: 在解剖学准确的主动脉模型上成功演示了该装置的导航能力，验证了其在复杂血管环境中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该手持式驱动机构能够在保持临床医生操作习惯的同时，提供机器人化的导丝转向能力，有望降低血管介入手术中的操作风险和相关并发症。

Abstract: An endovascular intervention begins with a skilled clinician manually navigating a long, slender wire, called a guidewire, to the target location within the vasculature. Due to factors, such as vessel tortuosity and lack of steerability at the guidewire tip, manual navigation of a guidewire could be challenging, potentially resulting in vessel damage, perforation, dissection, and occlusion, as well as postsurgical complications, such as thrombosis. This work details the development of a handheld actuation mechanism for tendon-driven robotic guidewires by utilizing the aforementioned spooling mechanism. Incorporating the compact spooling mechanism into a handheld device enables clinicians to leverage both motorized guidewire steering and manual rotation and translation, if desired. The proposed device is designed such that, while holding the device, the clinician can execute feeding, rotation, and bending motions for up to 1.5 m of the robotically steerable guidewire using a joystick and momentary switch on the handle. Furthermore, the proposed device is demonstrated through navigation in an anatomically accurate phantom aorta model.

</details>


### [183] [Data Pyramid for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24744)
*Yifan Ye,Yankai Fu,Yaoxu Lv,Bohan Hou,Jun Cen,Lingdong Kong,Duo Zheng,Tianxing Chen,Jiaming Liu,Ziang Cao,Yunfan Lou,Wei Chow,Xian Sun,Yingshuo Wang,Kuangzhi Ge,Xiaowei Chi,Xidong Zhang,Zhibo Pang,Yiwu Zhong,Sirui Han,Zhihe Lu,Weihao Yuan,Qifeng Chen,Michael Yu Wang,Yao Mu,Ziwei Liu,Jianfei Yang,Ping Luo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了具身智能领域的数据生态系统，提出了一个涵盖五类数据源（真实机器人数据、UMI风格数据、自我中心与外中心数据、仿真数据、通用视觉-语言数据）的“金字塔”框架，并分析了当前具身基础模型的数据配方及其对模型能力的影响，最后指出了六个未解决的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体无法像多模态基础模型那样直接从互联网数据中学习，因为其需要将观测与物理状态和动作耦合的数据。目前尚无系统性工作梳理具身数据的来源及其在模型训练中的角色与挑战。

Method: 构建“数据金字塔”框架，从可扩展性与机器人对齐的张力出发，以数据质量、多样性、可复用性和物理保真度四个维度刻画五类数据源；并通过数据配方（数据选择、对齐、混合）的视角分析现有具身基础模型（脑模型、视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型）的能力来源。

Result: 揭示了不同数据源在感知、推理、规划、动作生成和世界预测等能力上的贡献差异；指出了当前具身数据生态中六项开放挑战：大规模触觉数据集、失败与恢复数据、可扩展数据采集流程、跨形态动作对齐、自我中心数据用于灵巧操作、以及原则性的数据配方设计。

Conclusion: 本文为下一代具身智能系统的数据设计奠定了基础，强调了在可扩展性与机器人对齐之间取得平衡是构建具身基础模型的核心难题。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models learned to see and to speak by consuming the whole internet. Embodied agents admit no such shortcut, since they require data that couple observations with physical states and actions. These signals can be provided, to varying degrees, by multiple data sources. In this work, we organize the embodied data ecosystem as a "pyramid" spanning five complementary sources: real-robot data, UMI-style data, egocentric and exocentric data, simulation data, and general vision-language data. We organize the pyramid around the tension between scalability and robot alignment, and further characterize each source in terms of data quality, diversity, reusability, and physical fidelity. We then analyze recent embodied foundation models through the lens of their data recipes, examining how different sources are selected, aligned, and mixed during pretraining. For embodied brain models, vision-language-action models, and world-action models alike, we relate data composition to capabilities in perception, reasoning, planning, action generation, and world prediction. We close by discussing six open challenges: building large-scale tactile datasets, collecting failure and recovery data, developing scalable data-collection pipelines, aligning actions across embodiments, leveraging egocentric data for dexterous manipulation, and designing principled data recipes for robot learning. We hope this work paves the foundation for the design of next-generation embodied systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C-VCE，一种将分类器内置到扩散模型概念瓶颈层中的框架，通过可解释概念引导生成反事实解释，无需依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器，在保持编辑真实性的同时实现更小、更可控的图像修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的反事实解释方法依赖外部分类器，但外部分类器需要在噪声图像上可靠工作，这使其脆弱且难以部署用于鲁棒的解释。

Method: C-VCE通过概念瓶颈层将分类器直接构建到生成模型中，利用人类可解释特征（概念）引导反事实生成；引入概率正则化项平衡"改变预测"与"保持与原图接近"，并通过梯度掩码将修改限制在最相关的区域。

Result: 在CelebA等基准测试上，C-VCE达到或改进了翻转率，同时生成的反事实在视觉上更接近输入图像，失真更小，优于依赖独立噪声图像分类器的基线方法。

Conclusion: 暴露和控制内部概念层是使强大生成模型更易理解、更安全使用的有前景的方法，C-VCE为需要具体"假设"图像的视觉系统提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [185] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeT-Diff 是一种基于扩散模型的基础模型，通过将生成过程条件化为传感器语义描述，实现了对HPC计算节点遥测数据的高精度重建、预测和虚拟传感，在真实超算数据集上达到0.0470 MAE的重建误差，并具备零样本排列稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC遥测机器学习方法依赖静态、固定位置的传感器变量子集，当目标任务变化或传感器指标变动时模型失效，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出SeT-Diff，基于扩散模型的生成式架构，将生成过程条件化为每个传感器的语义描述，使系统动力学与数据集结构解耦，实现跨任务泛化。

Result: 真实超算数据集上重建任务MAE为0.0470；零样本排列稳定性验证中传感器打乱后精度几乎无下降；单个预训练模型可执行数据插补、预测和虚拟传感，热推断任务MAE为0.033。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff首次将扩散模型用于计算节点遥测时间序列，作为数据驱动的数字孪生体，具备高精度、灵活性和泛化能力，适用于HPC系统的多种下游任务。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [186] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QFoldAgent是一个闭环多智能体框架，用于5残基四面体晶格蛋白质结构预测。它通过设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚项、VQE量子-经典流水线优化哈密顿量、反馈智能体基于能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代调整惩罚权重。在QDockBank基准上，中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å；在未见序列上，结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，87%的初始无效案例得以恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于晶格的量子-经典蛋白质结构预测方法通常人为固定哈密顿量惩罚权重，且仅在极短片段上进行仿真评估，缺乏系统性的权重优化机制，导致预测精度和鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出QFoldAgent闭环多智能体框架：(1) 设计智能体根据序列生成条件化的惩罚权重；(2) VQE量子-经典流水线在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量；(3) 反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代调整惩罚参数。仅在评估阶段使用RMSD等真实指标。

Result: QDockBank基准上中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å，最难目标上提升最大。未见序列上结构有效性从87.5%升至98.7%，87%初始无效案例恢复；最强控制器在87%序列上改善了cycle-3能量，同时保持96%的Ramachandran偏好几何。

Conclusion: 迭代智能体控制能够系统地改善5残基量子设置下的优化行为并减少失败案例，证明了闭环多智能体方法在量子-经典蛋白质结构预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [187] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在不同但等价的提问方式下答案不稳定，单次提示的正确性无法可靠衡量模型能力，通过自释义策略可部分恢复潜在知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率指标无法反映LLM在等价表述下的回答一致性，现有评估忽视了模型对措辞变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 在4个基准和13个模型上，对事实问答和数学推理任务进行保义释义变换，测量模型答案的变化，并提出自释义策略来恢复潜在知识。

Result: 整体准确率变化不大，但实例级行为不稳定；原问题正确时错翻率更高，答案翻转率超过23%；多数问题至少有一种释义能给出正确答案。

Conclusion: 标准准确率掩盖了实质性不稳定性，评估等价输入下的一致性才能更清晰反映LLM的可靠性，自释义策略可有效提升推理时性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [188] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepLenz诊断智能体，一个五阶段流水线（结构化临床提取、约束检索、候选生成、证据三角验证、可审计决策），基于小型医疗推理模型JSL Medical Small 7B v2和RAG。在DiagnosisArena基准上达到60.14%的top-1诊断准确率，相较于无智能体流程的同一模型（23.99%）提升36个点，且成本仅0.0072美元/病例，低于Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro的同时性能领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿LLM虽为强通用模型，但单次提示在医疗诊断推理中往往产生脆弱的推理结果，缺乏结构化流程约束；同时，小型模型虽在标准医疗基准上表现良好（88.2%），但在不确定性下的诊断推理能力不足，说明知识召回不等于诊断推理。

Method: 提出五阶段诊断流水线：1) 结构化临床提取（提取事实）；2) 约束检索（基于RAG的知识查询）；3) 候选生成（约束下的鉴别诊断生成）；4) 显式证据三角验证（证据交叉验证）；5) 可审计最终决策。核心模型为JSL Medical Small 7B v2，结合模型能力与过程约束。

Result: 在915例DiagnosisArena上，智能体达到60.14% top-1准确率，无流程的同一模型仅23.99%（+36点提升）；成本0.0072美元/病例（24K tokens，A100），延迟24秒；比Claude Sonnet 4.5便宜35-45%且性能高9.70pp，比Gemini 3.1 Pro便宜且高9.17pp。流程还可纠正前沿模型的失败案例。

Conclusion: 流程约束比参数规模或API成本更能提升诊断性能；结构化中间产物使各阶段可检查、可定位错误，支持高 stakes 场景下的可追溯性、可复现性和可审计性。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [189] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIITA提出了一种记忆驱动的推理时自适应框架，通过存储压缩修正方向原型和语义锚点，在固定存储预算下实现资源受限小语言模型的持续学习，无需更新主干网络或扩展提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）参数空间有限，直接更新会导致灾难性遗忘；现有面向大语言模型的基于记忆的方法依赖大量存储和强上下文推理能力，SLM无法满足这些条件。

Method: 将监督经验存储为带语义锚点的压缩修正方向原型；推理时基于语义和不确定性线索检索，通过门控临时隐藏状态适配应用检索到的方向，实现无破坏性的过去监督复用，无需主干更新、提示扩展或测试时反向传播。

Result: 在多种监督持续学习场景下，MIITA在固定记忆预算内持续提升最终性能并有效缓解遗忘。

Conclusion: MIITA通过记忆驱动的推理时适配机制，有效解决了资源受限场景下小语言模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，性能优于现有方法。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues. The retrieved directions are applied through gated temporary hidden-state adaptation, enabling non-destructive reuse of past supervision without backbone updates, prompt extensions, or test-time backpropagation. A local theoretical analysis links this design to first-order loss reduction, uncertainty-guided retrieval, and directional coverage for retaining old-stage knowledge. Extensive experiments across diverse supervised CL settings show that MIITA consistently improves final performance and mitigates forgetting under fixed memory budgets.

</details>


### [190] [Codifying the Judge: Scalable Evaluation via Program Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22561)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Shengqi Qiu,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAJAMA系统，通过将LLM的判决逻辑蒸馏为程序委员会，实现透明、高效、低成本的自动评估，在五个数据集上达到13B模型性能，并显著降低API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge虽已成为自动化评估标准，但存在高成本、显著延迟和决策不透明等问题，限制了其可扩展性和可靠性。作者旨在寻找一个简单、高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出程序蒸馏（program distillation）方法，将LLM的判决逻辑蒸馏为可执行的程序委员会。系统PAJAMA包含：1) 合成程序作为评判者；2) 聚合多个程序的决策形成联合裁决；3) 对低置信度案例选择性回退到LLM的fallback机制。

Result: 在五个数据集和四个模型家族上，程序化评判者匹配了13B规模LLM评判者的性能。PAJAMA提升了准确率和吞吐量，推进了帕累托前沿。在RewardBench上，从程序裁决蒸馏的奖励模型在低两个数量级的API成本下超越了基于专有LLM标签训练的模型。

Conclusion: 程序蒸馏是一种简单有效的替代LLM-as-a-judge的方案，兼具透明度、可编辑性、低延迟和低成本优势，在评估和奖励信号两方面均表现优异。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [191] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SF-AMS 提出了一种基于效用驱动的记忆遗忘框架，通过建模记忆单元的长时重要性，替代静态检索和启发式衰减，实现紧凑、高可用的智能体记忆管理，在多跳推理、时序推理和开放域任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在处理长上下文依赖时，冗余和无关信息会严重退化多步推理能力，现有记忆系统依赖静态检索或启发式衰减，缺乏对记忆长期动态重要性的建模。

Method: 提出 Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) 框架，核心包括：(1) 效用驱动的生存机制，通过使用冗余度和时序信号动态更新记忆重要性；(2) 层次化记忆结构，优先保留稳定的实体一致性信息并过滤噪声；(3) Composite Importance Scoring，融合语义级和实体级信号以增强检索鲁棒性。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-s 上一致超越 LightMem、MemO、A-Mem 等强基线。Qwen2.5-7B 下多跳推理 F1 提升 +9.65，GPT-4o-mini 下时序推理 F1 提升 +6.91，开放域任务提升 +6.53，表现出强跨骨干网络泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号对于可靠的长期上下文推理至关重要，SF-AMS 验证了该思路在多种任务和模型上的有效性。

Abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem. The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [192] [Synthetic Scenario Generation for Evaluation of Industry 4.0 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22563)
*Sagar Chethan Kumar,Rohith Kanathur,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScenarioGeneratorAgent 流水线，用于在工业智能体基准测试中合成生成基于标准（IEC）的场景。通过针对智能电网变压器资产类别的扩展，结合多级缓存、并行生成等优化，实现了在保证场景质量的前提下 8 倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业智能体基准（如 AssetOpsBench）依赖人工编写场景，覆盖资产类别有限，缺乏大规模、可扩展的场景生成方法，难以满足真实工业评估需求。

Method: 提出 ScenarioGeneratorAgent 流水线，包含：(1) 证据驱动的资产画像构建；(2) 覆盖感知的场景预算分配；(3) 混合验证-修复循环（支持模式有效性、工具可达性、物理合理性、标准对齐、去重）；(4) 两阶段缓存、并行焦点组生成、线程池卸载、批量 LLM 调用、早期拒绝过滤等优化策略。

Result: 在智能电网变压器场景生成上，优化后端到端运行时间减少 8 倍（生成 50 个场景），复合质量评分 74.2±1.9，与未优化基线 73.8±3.0 相当，未牺牲质量。

Conclusion: 基于标准引导的合成场景生成能够高效扩展工业智能体基准，且保持场景质量，为工业基准的自动化扩充提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation. The pipeline constructs evidence-grounded asset profiles, allocates coverage-aware scenario budgets across operational domains, and generates candidates through a hybrid validation-and-repair loop that enforces schema validity, tool reachability, physical plausibility, standards alignment, and deduplication. To improve scalability, we apply two-level caching, parallel focus-group generation, thread-pool offloading, batched LLM calls, and early rejection filtering. On Smart Grid Transformer scenario generation, these optimizations reduce end-to-end runtime by $8\times$ for 50 scenarios while preserving quality, achieving a composite quality score of $74.2 \pm 1.9$ compared with $73.8 \pm 3.0$ for the unoptimized baseline. These results show that standards-grounded synthetic scenario generation can efficiently expand industrial-agent benchmarks without sacrificing scenario quality.

</details>


### [193] [Loss-Aware Feature-Map Pruning in Convolutional Neural Networks Using Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22564)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多臂赌博机的损失感知特征图剪枝框架，将每个候选特征图视为臂，通过临时遮蔽并评估损失变化来生成安全移除奖励，最终永久移除Top-k特征图及其对应滤波器、偏置和下一层输入通道核。实验表明UCB1和Thompson Sampling在显著减少计算量的同时保持了接近未剪枝模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络中常存在冗余特征图，增加了存储和推理成本，需要一种结构化的、损失感知的剪枝方法来移除这些冗余特征图及其对应的滤波器。

Method: 采用多臂赌博机框架，每个候选特征图视为一个臂；每次迭代时临时遮蔽一个特征图并在小批量数据上评估，观察损失变化并转换为安全移除奖励；经过固定预算后，根据学习到的分数永久移除Top-k特征图及其相关参数。

Result: UCB1和Thompson Sampling在多个数据集上保持了接近未剪枝模型的精度，同时显著减少了特征图和卷积计算量；Friedman和Nemenyi检验表明UCB1平均秩最高，Thompson Sampling次之，两者显著优于贪心和基于幅度的剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 基于多臂赌博机的损失感知剪枝能够有效识别并移除冗余特征图，在保持精度的同时降低计算成本，UCB1和Thompson Sampling是有效的剪枝策略。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks often contain redundant feature maps that increase storage and inference cost. This paper presents a loss-aware feature-map pruning framework using multi-armed bandits. Feature-map pruning is structured because it removes complete convolutional output channels and their producing filters rather than isolated scalar weights. Each candidate feature map is treated as an arm. At each play time, one map is temporarily masked and evaluated on a sampled mini-batch; the map is then restored and the observed loss change is converted into a safe-removal reward. After a fixed play budget, candidate maps are ranked by learned scores and the top-k maps are permanently removed with their filters, biases and corresponding next-layer input-channel kernels. The study evaluates UCB1 and Thompson Sampling, compares them with direct/oracle-style evaluation on LeNet/MNIST, and extends the evaluation to MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, CUB-200-2011 and Oxford Flowers 102. Results show that UCB1 and Thompson Sampling preserve accuracy close to unpruned models while removing feature maps and reducing convolutional computation. Friedman and Nemenyi tests show that UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank, followed by Thompson Sampling; both significantly outperform greedy and magnitude-based pruning while remaining statistically comparable to the original unpruned model.

</details>


### [194] [DSTFView: Multi-View Cloud-Edge Workload Forecasting with Dual-Input Spatio-Temporal-Frequency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22565)
*Qingzhong Li,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Qingchang Ma,Zhou Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSTFView框架，通过双输入时空频多视图协同建模，在云边协同环境下实现工作负载预测，在CPU和TP数据集上优于主流基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端AI推理广泛部署，现有方法难以在云边协同环境中平衡多维特征建模与预测效率。

Method: 提出DSTFView，一种双输入时空频多视图工作负载预测框架，联合建模邻近与周期依赖，提取时空频域依赖，并设计自适应融合机制动态调整各视图贡献以捕捉突变。

Result: 在CPU和TP数据集上，DSTFView在多个预测步长和评估指标上一致优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: DSTFView有效解决了云边协同环境下工作负载预测的多维特征建模难题，具备良好的预测精度与适应性。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of edge-side AI inference, edge platforms are increasingly required to support latency-sensitive, highly concurrent, and reliability-critical applications. However, existing methods often struggle to balance multidimensional feature modeling and forecasting efficiency in collaborative cloud-edge environments. To address this issue, we propose DSTFView, a dual-input spatio-temporal-frequency multi-view workload forecasting framework for collaborative cloud-edge environments. It jointly models closeness and period dependencies and extracts spatial, temporal, and frequency-domain dependencies. Besides, it designs an adaptive fusion mechanism and adjusts the contribution of each view to capture abrupt changes. Experimental results on the CPU and TP datasets demonstrate that DSTFView consistently outperforms representative baselines across multiple forecasting horizons and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [195] [MedLoCoMo: A Long-Context Multi-Session Medical Dialogue Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22566)
*Zeyu Zhang,Ziqing Wang,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedLoCoMo是一个面向多入院医疗对话的医学长上下文记忆基准，包含100个患者时间线（平均1669.8轮对话、29.7次就诊、74,512.2 tokens），测试模型在单次入院、跨入院及对抗性不可回答三种场景下的患者级临床推理能力。实验表明跨入院推理始终比局部证据使用更难，即使模型具备长上下文窗口或外部记忆/检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学QA基准主要测试短上下文知识或单文档定位，缺乏评估LLM在纵向患者历史中使用、连接和拒答能力的基准，因此需要构建多入院长上下文医学对话记忆基准。

Method: 基于去标识化的MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-IV-Note记录，构建入院级临床数据包，合成医患对话，生成三类QA项：单次入院、跨入院和对抗性不可回答问题。

Result: 跨入院推理始终比局部证据使用更困难，即使使用长上下文窗口或外部记忆/检索方法的长上下文模型也难以克服这一挑战。

Conclusion: MedLoCoMo填补了医学长上下文记忆基准的空白，揭示了当前LLM在跨入院临床推理中的局限性，为该方向的改进提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: MedLoCoMo is a Medical Long-Context Memory benchmark for patient-specific clinical reasoning over multi-admission medical dialogue. Existing medical QA benchmarks largely test short context knowledge or single document grounding, leaving open whether LLMs can use, connect, and abstain over longitudinal patient histories. We build MedLoCoMo from deidentified MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-IV-Note records by constructing admission-level clinical packets, synthesizing grounded doctor-patient conversations, and generating evidence linked QA items over single-admission, cross-admission, and adversarial unanswerable settings. The benchmark contains 100 patient timelines averaging 1,669.8 turns, 29.7 sessions, and 74,512.2 tokens per conversation. Across the evaluated baselines, cross-admission reasoning is consistently harder than localized evidence use, even when models have long context windows or use external memory or retrieval methods. The code and MedLoCoMo benchmark release is available at https://github.com/leozzy13/MedLoCoMo for use and reproducibility.

</details>


### [196] [Keyword Matters: Unveiling the Energy Sensitivity of On-Device LLM Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22568)
*Ruiyi Tao,Xiaolong Tu,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过智能手机上的真实功耗测量，实证研究了提示词（prompt）的措辞对设备端大语言模型能量消耗的影响，发现不同的命令动词和指令结构会导致解码长度和总能耗的显著差异，表明提示工程可作为提升能效的轻量级手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在移动和嵌入式设备上部署时面临电池容量和硬件资源有限带来的高能耗约束，已有研究多聚焦于模型压缩和运行时加速，但提示词设计对能效的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在智能手机上收集真实功耗数据，通过操控提示词中的语言特征（特别是命令式关键词和指令结构），量化这些特征对解码长度和总能耗的影响。

Result: 不同动词和任务之间存在一致的能耗差异，提示词措辞会显著影响解码长度和总能量消耗。

Conclusion: 提示工程可以作为提升设备端大语言模型能效的轻量级手段，为在资源受限硬件上部署LLM提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile and embedded devices to improve privacy and reduce network latency. Yet on-device inference faces a fundamental constraint: high energy consumption on battery-powered, resource-limited hardware. While model compression and runtime acceleration have been widely studied, the effect of \emph{prompt design} on energy efficiency remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical study of the relationship between prompt wording and energy consumption for on-device LLMs. Using real power measurements collected on a smartphone, we quantify how linguistic features, particularly imperative keywords and instruction structure, affect decoding length and total energy. Our results show consistent energy differences across verbs and tasks, indicating that prompt engineering is a lightweight lever for improving energy efficiency.

</details>


### [197] [Execution-Grounded Security Testing for Coding Agents in Software Engineering Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22569)
*Yifei Ge,Weisong Sun,Jinkun Xiao,Yuchen Chen,Yebo Feng,Peizhuo Lv,Xia Feng,Chunrong Fang,Zhihong Zhao,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行层的红队测试框架，通过将不安全操作嵌入常规软件工程任务（单元测试、回归测试、崩溃复现、验证），利用工具调用、运行时追踪和文件系统差异等可观察沙箱证据，探测编码智能体在系统运维中的安全边界。实验表明，当恶意意图隐藏在合理工程任务中时，编码智能体在代码载体和文本载体上的不安全执行率分别达到73.61%和53.93%，揭示了当前编码智能体在系统运维中仍存在严重安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在系统运维中的工具使用可以直接修改项目工件、执行环境和底层系统，例如向系统启动脚本插入钩子后，该修改会持久存在、被后续触发，并可能滥用委托的用户或系统权限。这使安全测试成为一个系统问题：关键不仅是智能体说了什么，而是它对周围环境实际做了什么。现有安全测试方法不足以探测这种执行层面的安全边界。

Method: 提出执行层红队测试框架，将目标不安全操作嵌入常规软件工程工作负载（单元测试、回归测试、崩溃复现、验证），使用执行预言机（execution oracle）在初始探测被拒绝或失败时指导细化。通过可观察沙箱证据（工具调用、运行时追踪、文件系统差异）进行安全边界探测。

Result: 在多种智能体框架和模型骨干上，红队工作负载重构显著提高了已验证的不安全执行率，代码载体达到73.61%，文本载体达到53.93%。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在系统运维中在任务伪装下仍不安全：一旦风险意图隐藏在合理工程任务中，智能体可能被诱导向周围系统执行不安全操作。编码智能体在系统运维中仍需更强的安全测试和安全保障措施。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly integrated into system operations, where their tool use can directly modify project artifacts, execution environments, and the underlying system. For example, if a coding agent inserts a hook into a system startup or configuration script, that change can persist after the interaction, be triggered later, and abuse delegated user or system privileges to modify the system. This makes security testing a system problem: the key question is not only what the agent says, but what it actually does to the surrounding environment. We present an execution-grounded red-team testing framework for probing this execution-layer security boundary using observable sandbox evidence, including tool invocations, runtime traces, and file-system diffs. Our framework embeds target unsafe operations into routine software engineering workloads, including unit testing, regression testing, crash reproduction, and validation, and uses an execution oracle to guide refinement when an initial probe is rejected or fails. Across multiple agent frameworks and model backbones, our red-team workload reformulation substantially increases verified unsafe execution, reaching 73.61% on code carriers and 53.93% on text carriers. These results show that coding agents in system operations remain insecure under task disguise: once risky intent is hidden inside plausible engineering tasks, the agent can be induced to carry out unsafe actions on the surrounding system. More broadly, coding agents in system operations still demand stronger security testing and safeguards.

</details>


### [198] [Reference Feature Atlases for Mechanistic Auditing of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22570)
*Rui Wu,Tong Che*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出参考特征图谱法：在参考模型上训练稀疏特征库，新模型仅需线性解码器即可复用。通过图谱通道（共享坐标系）和残差通道（未重构信号作为审计信号）双视角审计。在五个7-9B模型上训练留一法图谱，残差通道能完全可控地检测植入的LoRA隐藏目标，而基线方法失败；在Qwen-2.5上还揭示了政治框架聚类，干预后定向改变审计指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次审计新语言模型都需要从头重新学习和解释其内部特征，缺乏可迁移的审计框架，成本高昂且难以跨模型比较。

Method: 1) 在参考模型面板上训练稀疏特征库（参考特征图谱）；2) 新目标模型通过拟合线性解码器附着到图谱上；3) 双视角：图谱通道用已有特征解读目标，残差通道捕获图谱无法重构的特征作为显式审计信号。

Result: 1) 在五个7-9B模型上训练留一法图谱，审计Mistral和Qwen目标；2) 残差通道使植入的LoRA隐藏目标完全可控，基线（逐目标SAE和成对交叉编码器）失败；3) Qwen-2.5上发现政治框架聚类，干预后定向改变审计指标。

Conclusion: 参考特征图谱法实现了跨模型的稳定特征坐标系复用，残差通道提供了一种高效的、可迁移的审计新机制，能够检测隐藏目标和未知特征模式。

Abstract: Auditing a new language model usually means relearning and reinterpreting its internal features from scratch. We propose a reference feature atlas: a sparse feature library trained once on a reference panel and reused for new targets, which attach by fitting only a linear decoder. This yields two complementary views. The atlas channel reads the target on already interpreted panel features, providing a stable coordinate system across models. The residual channel learns features only from what the atlas fails to reconstruct, making "outside the reference panel" an explicit audit signal.
  We train leave-one-out atlases over five 7-9B instruction-tuned models and audit held-out Mistral and Qwen targets. On three controlled LoRA hidden objectives injected into both targets, the residual channel makes the planted mechanism perfectly controllable at runtime while matched controls stay unaffected and recovers the planted objective as the top-ranked latent across both targets; on Mistral, where the per-target SAE and pairwise crosscoder baselines are retrained for a head-to-head benchmark, both baselines fail to do so. On Qwen-2.5, the same channel additionally reveals a panel-relative political-framing cluster; steering it shifts the audited framing metrics while out-of-domain controls remain unchanged.

</details>


### [199] [SCAIR: Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning for Enterprise Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22571)
*Prateek Chaturvedi,Yuqicheng Zhu,Hongkuan Zhou,Dongzhuoran Zhou,Yunjie He,Steffen Staab,Fei Du,Jie Tang,Evgeny Kharlamov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCAIR是一种无需训练的框架，通过注入Schema条件结构先验并执行Schema感知的多跳推理，解决了现有KG-RAG方法在企业级知识图谱（密集、Schema驱动、操作受限）上泛化能力差的问题。在真实CMDB构建的企业基准测试上，SCAIR显著优于现有方法，证明了企业图推理必须显式融入目标领域的结构与操作约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Agent的KG-RAG方法在公开基准上表现良好，但在真实企业知识图谱（密集、Schema驱动、操作受限）上泛化能力差。

Method: 提出SCAIR（Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning），一种无训练框架，通过注入Schema条件结构先验并强制进行Schema感知的多跳推理，将结构化规划与受控迭代推理相结合。

Result: 在基于真实世界CMDB构建的企业级基准上，SCAIR显著优于现有KG-RAG方法。

Conclusion: 可靠的企业级图推理不能依赖通用Agent设计，必须显式融入目标领域的结构与操作约束；通过将Agent设计与业务逻辑对齐，可以在无需昂贵模型重训练的情况下实现显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG) enables natural language interaction with structured enterprise knowledge, yet existing agentic approaches that perform well on public benchmarks often fail to generalize to real-world enterprise Knowledge Graphs (KGs), which are dense, schema-driven, and operationally constrained. To address these limitations, we propose SCAIR (Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning), a training-free framework that integrates structured planning with controlled iterative reasoning by injecting schema-conditioned structural priors and enforcing schema-aware traversal during multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on an enterprise-oriented benchmark constructed from a real-world Configuration Management DataBase (CMDB) demonstrate that SCAIR substantially improves performance over existing KG-RAG methods. Crucially, our study highlights that reliable enterprise graph reasoning cannot rely on generic agentic designs; instead, it must explicitly incorporate the target domain's structural and operational constraints into the reasoning process. We demonstrate that by aligning agent design with business logic, substantial performance gains can be achieved without the need for costly model retraining.

</details>


### [200] [Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL): Live Schema Grounding and Hallucination Validation for Oracle NL2SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22572)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL)，一种轻量级中间件，通过实时查询Oracle数据字典构建模式映射、注入LLM提示并利用Hallucination Index验证别名引用，在无需模型重训练的情况下将TPC-H数据集上的执行成功率从2.2%提升至62.6%，执行失败率从97.6%降至2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在真实企业Oracle数据库上生成SQL时频繁出现执行失败，主要原因是列名和别名的幻觉（hallucination）以及方言特定语法缺失，导致ORA-00904无效标识符错误；模型无法获知数据库中实际存在的表和列信息，缺乏模式接地（schema grounding）是关键问题。

Method: SAL包含两个核心组件：（1）实时模式映射构建——查询Oracle的USER_TAB_COLUMNS数据字典，为每个自然语言问题选择相关表子集（多表查询时回退到完整模式），并将真实上下文注入LLM提示；（2）Hallucination Index (Hidx)——对生成SQL中的每个alias.column引用进行实时校验，自动重写可预测的前缀错误，否则触发结构化重试并附带逐项修正。

Result: 在500条TPC-H自然语言问题、Oracle Autonomous Database 23c、GPT-4o-mini设置下：无模式接地时EGT为2.2%（12/500）；手写静态模式提示达62.0%；SAL无需人工模式筛选即达62.6% EGT（简单96%、中等95%、复杂40.7%），执行失败率从97.6%降至2.6%。

Conclusion: SAL通过轻量级、无需模型重训练的中间件方案，有效解决了LLM在真实Oracle数据库上SQL生成中的模式幻觉问题，显著提升了执行成功率和结果正确性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent SQL from natural language, but on real enterprise Oracle databases they frequently fail at execution time: columns and aliases are hallucinated and dialect-specific syntax is missed, leading to ORA-00904 invalid-identifier errors. In this setting, failures are primarily due to missing schema grounding: the model cannot know which tables and columns actually exist. This paper introduces Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL), a lightweight middleware layer for Oracle NL2SQL that requires no model retraining. SAL queries Oracle's USER_TAB_COLUMNS catalog to build a live schema map, selects a relevant table subset for each question (falling back to the full schema for multi-table queries), and injects this ground-truth context into the LLM prompt. Generated SQL is then checked by the Hallucination Index (Hidx), which validates every alias.column reference against the live catalog, automatically rewrites predictable prefix errors, and otherwise triggers a structured retry with itemised corrections. We evaluate SAL on 500 TPC-H natural language questions executed against a live Oracle Autonomous Database 23c instance using GPT-4o-mini. Without any schema grounding, execution-grounded truth (EGT; executes and matches the reference result set) is 2.2% (12/500). A hand-written static schema hint brings EGT to 62.0%. SAL, with no manual schema curation, achieves 62.6% EGT (96% simple, 95% medium, 40.7% complex) while reducing execution failures from 97.6% to 2.6%.

</details>


### [201] [PhononBench-MP40: a spectrum-resolved benchmark dataset for phonon stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22573)
*Wen-Kao Li,Ze-Feng Gao,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PhononBench-MP40数据集，包含46,899条Materials Project衍生晶体的声子稳定性记录（16,683条稳定，30,216条不稳定），基于局部声子YAML谱提供工作流定义的稳定性标签，并公开在Science Data Bank。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚频声子模是计算材料筛选中因工作流依赖导致的局部动态不稳定的实际瓶颈，现有数据缺乏带谱解析的基准集和失效感知的标签体系。

Method: 从47,969个MP40工作流任务出发，完成46,899条记录，每条包含局部phonopy YAML谱、稳定性标签、最低采样频率及阈值重标定，并单独报告1,067条弛豫失效。

Result: 数据集含16,683条Stable和30,216条completed-phonon不稳定记录，公开在Science Data Bank并附GitHub工具库。

Conclusion: PhononBench-MP40为工作流定义的稳定性分类、最低频分析、阈值研究与失效分类提供可审计基准，同时明确工作流、数据模式与解释边界。

Abstract: Imaginary phonon modes remain a practical bottleneck in computational materials screening because otherwise plausible structures can be locally dynamically unstable under a chosen workflow. Here we present PhononBench-MP40, a spectrum-resolved benchmark dataset of Materials Project-derived crystals for workflow-defined phonon stability. The dataset starts from 47,969 MP40 workflow tasks and provides 46,899 completed records with paired stability labels and local phonopy YAML spectra, including 16,683 Stable records and 30,216 completed-phonon unstable records. A further 1,067 relaxation failures are reported separately rather than merged into the completed phonon denominator. The release centers on the local YAML spectrum: the stability label, the lowest sampled frequency and any threshold-dependent relabeling are derived from that spectrum. The dataset is openly available through Science Data Bank at https://doi.org/10.57760/sciencedb.38735. A companion GitHub repository provides the calculation code and lightweight access utilities. PhononBench-MP40 provides an auditable reference for workflow-defined stability classification, minimum-frequency analysis, threshold studies and failure-aware triage, while keeping the reference workflow, data schema and interpretation boundaries explicit.

</details>


### [202] [Too much evidence, too little time: From text to actionable recommendations through multi-objective evidence reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22574)
*Adela Bara,Simona-Vasilica Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCEPTER是一个将临床病例描述转化为循证推荐意见的框架，结合PubMed检索、语义排序、大语言模型、证据级别加权、矛盾检测和帕累托多目标优化，将平均576篇文献压缩为53篇保留论文、7个帕累托最优主张和3条最终推荐（压缩比192:1），同时保持证据多样性（熵=0.901）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于证据的临床决策需要专家识别、评估和综合相关科学文献，但PubMed检索复杂病例时往往返回数百篇文献，无法在时间约束下人工审阅。

Method: SCEPTER框架结合了PubMed检索、PubMedBERT语义排序、基于大语言模型的主张提取、证据级别加权、矛盾检测、共识分析和多目标帕累托主张选择，并包含Paper Q&A模块支持交互式文献探索。

Result: 在150个病例研究上评估，将平均576篇文献减少至53篇保留论文、7个帕累托最优主张和3条最终推荐，整体压缩比192:1，保留证据多样性高（熵=0.901）。消融实验表明帕累托选择相比传统排序方法提升了证据多样性和推荐效用。

Conclusion: SCEPTER引入了一种多目标推理模型，将文献支持、矛盾分析和交互式文献查询整合到统一的临床决策支持流程中，有效压缩搜索空间并保持证据多样性。

Abstract: Evidence-based clinical decision making requires specialists to identify, evaluate and synthesize relevant scientific literature. However, PubMed searches for complex clinical cases often return hundreds of publications that cannot be reviewed manually under time constraints. This study proposes SCEPTER (Single-Case Evidence-driven PubMed-To-rEcommendation Reasoner), a framework for transforming clinical case descriptions into evidence-based recommendations. SCEPTER combines PubMed retrieval, PubMedBERT semantic ranking, large language model (LLM)-based claim extraction, evidence-level weighting, contradiction detection, consensus analysis and multi-objective Pareto claim selection. The framework generates structured evidence syntheses and grounded actionable recommendations. A Paper Q&A module further enables interactive exploration of selected publications. The proposed framework introduces multi-objective reasoning model that integrates literature support, contradiction analysis and interactive literature interrogation into a unified clinical decision-support pipeline. Evaluation on 150 case studies demonstrated that the framework reduced an average search space of 576 papers to 53 retained papers, 7 Pareto-optimal claims and 3 final recommendations, corresponding to an overall compression ratio of 192:1. Despite this reduction, the retained evidence maintained high diversity (entropy=0.901). The ablation study showed that Pareto-based selection increased evidence diversity and recommendation utility compared with conventional ranking approaches.

</details>


### [203] [Temporal Context Reinstatement Drives Episodic-Like Order Memory in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22575)
*Mathis Pink,Vy Ai Vo,Qinyuan Wu,Jianing Mu,Javier Turek,Uri Hasson,Kenneth A. Norman,Sebastian Michelmann,Alexander Huth,Mariya Toneva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过长上下文大语言模型（LLMs）模拟人类情景记忆的时间顺序任务，发现模型表现出与人类相同的距离效应，并通过机制可解释性分析揭示模型依赖单个注意力头实现一维时间编码的重新激活来完成任务，支持时间情境重新激活作为情景记忆的重要机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类情景记忆能够检索跨越较长时间尺度的经验，但其计算机制因人类长期记忆实验的机制可解释性有限而存在争议，因此需要借助长上下文LLMs来揭示可能的计算机制。

Method: 使用基于整部长篇小说记忆的人类行为新数据集，通过时间顺序记忆任务比较人类与LLMs的行为表现；随后运用长上下文机制可解释性分析方法，识别模型完成该任务的关键注意力头及其编码机制。

Result: 1) LLMs在时间顺序记忆任务中表现出与人类相同的特征性距离效应；2) 模型性能依赖于一维时间编码，该编码在检索阶段通过单个时间重新激活注意力头（time-reinstatement attention head）实现重新激活。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持时间情境重新激活（temporal context reinstatement）作为LLMs中类情景时间顺序记忆的重要机制，为理解人工系统和生物系统中长期情景记忆的时间方面如何实现提供了新见解。

Abstract: Human episodic memory supports the retrieval of experiences that unfold over extended timescales, yet the computational mechanisms underlying this ability remain debated due to the limited mechanistic accessibility in long-term memory experiments in humans. Long-context LLMs may offer promising ways to reveal plausible computational mechanisms that drive this type of retrieval. Here, we investigate whether and how LLMs capture the core behavioral signatures of episodic memory via a temporal order memory task. Using a new dataset of human behavior based on memory of a full-length novel, we show that models exhibit the same characteristic distance effect observed in humans on this task. We next apply long-context mechanistic interpretability analyses to uncover how models solve this task, and find that model performance relies on a one-dimensional temporal code that is reinstated during retrieval by a single time-reinstatement attention head. These findings support temporal context reinstatement as an important mechanism for episodic-like temporal-order memory in LLMs, offering new insights into how temporal aspects of long-term episodic memory may be instantiated in both artificial and biological systems.

</details>


### [204] [cMoLLM at Scale: Horizontal Scaling Laws for Mixture-of-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22577)
*Xin Yang,Yemin Wang,Mingda Liu,Letian Li,Shuaishuai Cao,Zhengxiao He,Ryan Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出cMoLLM，一种基于卷积门控的混合大语言模型方法，通过将MoE混合层重新形式化为可变核动态卷积，实现对端到端流的路由，在匹配计算量的条件下相比ParaScale和AltUp基线提升了语言建模困惑度、GLUE和SQuAD准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密Transformer中容量与计算量紧密耦合，每激活一个参数就要处理所有token，导致模型规模接近万亿参数时训练和推理成本线性增长，亟需一种更高效的缩放容量的方法。

Method: 将MoE风格的混合层重新形式化为可变核动态卷积，每个专家对应1×1卷积核，路由实现输入条件化的核聚合；提出cMoLLM，通过完全可微的动态卷积对端到端流进行路由。

Result: 在FineWeb上训练的GPT-2风格模型中，cMoLLM在匹配计算量下提升了语言建模困惑度、GLUE和SQuAD准确率，流利用率更好，优化更稳定，缩放性能优于ParaScale和AltUp基线。

Conclusion: cMoLLM通过卷积门控的混合大模型方式，有效解决了传统MoE方法（ParaScale、AltUp）存在的开销大、路由同质化、梯度崩溃、适应性有限等问题，在容量缩放上展现了更优的性能和稳定性。

Abstract: Scaling large language models (LLMs) has driven their success, yet dense Transformers couple capacity and computation: every parameter is activated for every token, making training and inference costs grow linearly with model size-a critical bottleneck as models approach trillion-parameter regimes. We aim to scale capacity through MoE-style mixture throughout the LLM pipeline rather than only the FFN. Prior pipeline-level approaches include ParaScale, which introduces virtual tokens and parallel streams but incurs substantial overhead and suffers from homogenized routing and gradient collapse, and AltUp, which uses an auxiliary prediction branch but offers limited adaptivity and slow convergence. We establish that MoE-style mixture layers can be reformulated as variable-kernel dynamic convolutions, where each expert corresponds to a $1{\times}1$ convolutional kernel and routing implements input-conditioned kernel aggregation. Building on this equivalence, we introduce cMoLLM: a convolutionally gated mixture-of-LLMs that routes over end-to-end streams through fully differentiable dynamic convolution. In GPT-2-style models trained on FineWeb, cMoLLM improves language modeling perplexity and downstream GLUE and SQuAD accuracy under matched compute, with better stream utilization, more stable optimization, and favorable scaling compared to ParaScale- and AltUp-style baselines.

</details>


### [205] [HeraSys: Collaborative Serving of Multiple LLM Workflows via Fine-Grained End-to-End Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22578)
*Size Li,Zhiqing Tang,Hongrui Liang,Jianxiong Guo,Jiong Lou,Tian Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeraSys是一个面向高并发、多租户LLM工作流场景的服务系统，通过跨工作流节点合并复用、负载感知的联合调度以及资源倾斜机制，在保证低延迟的同时显著降低尾部延迟并提升系统吞吐量。实验表明，P99延迟降低2.17倍，吞吐量提升1.85倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要关注工作流内部（intra-workflow）优化，忽视了工作流之间（inter-workflow）的计算冗余和调度优化潜力，导致多租户并发场景下的端到端性能受限。

Method: 1) 结构节点合并与复用（去除跨工作流计算冗余）；2) 负载感知联合调度策略（同时评估查询间和查询内优先级）；3) 资源倾斜机制结合自适应批处理与流水线分解。

Result: P99延迟降低至原来的1/2.17（即降低约54%），服务吞吐量提升至1.85倍，且在严格延迟保证下表现稳定。

Conclusion: 跨工作流粒度的协同优化是提升LLM服务系统性能的关键方向，HeraSys通过细粒度编排和负载感知调度有效解决了多租户并发工作流场景下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: The proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) has shifted serving systems from processing isolated requests to orchestrating high-concurrency, multi-tenant agentic workflows. However, existing solutions typically prioritize intra-workflow optimization, largely neglecting the significant potential for inter-workflow optimization. In this paper, we propose HeraSys, an LLM serving system designed to optimize the end-to-end performance of concurrent workflows. Through fine-grained orchestration, HeraSys eliminates cross-workflow computational redundancy via structural node merging and reuse. Furthermore, HeraSys introduces a load-aware joint scheduling policy that dynamically manages execution order by evaluating both inter- and intra-query priorities. By integrating a resource skewing mechanism with adaptive batching and pipeline decomposition, HeraSys effectively mitigates tail latency while maintaining low average latency, thereby substantially improving system throughput. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HeraSys reduces P99 latency by up to 2.17$\times$ and increases serving throughput by up to 1.85$\times$ under strict latency guarantees.

</details>


### [206] [Multi-Objective Structured Pruning of LLMs for Latency and Model Size Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22583)
*Muhammad Junaid Ali,Smail Niar,El-Ghazali Talbi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种硬件感知的多目标结构化剪枝框架，通过粗粒度深度剪枝和细粒度并行贝叶斯优化两阶段方法，在边缘设备上实现了LLM的高效部署，在37.5%和50%剪枝率下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在边缘计算环境中面临严格的延迟、内存和能量约束，而现有剪枝方法在联合优化层、注意力头和MLP维度时计算成本高且易陷入局部最优或不稳定配置。

Method: 两阶段结构化剪枝框架：粗粒度阶段进行多目标深度剪枝移除完整的注意力块和MLP块；细粒度阶段使用并行贝叶斯优化（PBO）搜索最优逐层剪枝比例，再结合重要性策略确定层内具体剪枝组件。

Result: 在37.5%和50%剪枝率下，该方法在常识推理任务上优于现有方法，同时显著降低推理成本，实现了精度、延迟和模型大小之间的有利权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法能在最小化对常识推理和零样本性能影响的前提下降低模型复杂度，适合边缘设备部署。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved widespread adoption because of their strong reasoning and query-response capabilities. However, deploying them in embedded and edge computing environments remains challenging because of strict latency, memory, and energy constraints. Their large parameter counts and computational demands hinder efficient execution on resource-constrained platforms. Although model pruning has emerged as a viable solution for reducing scale while preserving performance, jointly optimizing layers, attention heads, and Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) dimensions remains highly complex. Exhaustively exploring this combined design space is computationally expensive and often leads to local optima or unstable configurations. To address these limitations, we propose a hardware-aware, multi-objective structured pruning framework. The proposed two-stage method explicitly targets latency and model size for efficient deployment on edge devices. In the coarse-grained stage, multi-objective depth pruning removes entire attention and MLP blocks to reduce computational load and memory usage. In the subsequent fine-grained stage, Parallel Bayesian Optimization (PBO) searches for the optimal layer-wise pruning ratios for pruning under latency constraints, while importance-based strategies rank the specific components to be pruned within each layer's allocated budget. Experimental results show that our approach reduces model complexity with minimal impact on commonsense reasoning tasks and zero-shot performance. Our method achieves a favorable trade-off among accuracy, latency, and model size, making it suitable for edge deployment. Across multiple LLMs at 37.5% and 50% pruning ratios, the proposed approach achieves better performance on commonsense reasoning tasks than existing methods while significantly reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [207] [Source-Aware Reranking for Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Reliability Prior Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22584)
*Yuktha Tata Koganti,Hugo Garrido-Lestache Belinchon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于来源可信度先验的检索重排序方法，通过将语义相似度与来源先验因子相乘来改进RAG管道的检索质量，在120篇健康文档数据集中将Precision@5从0.48提升至0.72，并降低了对抗性文档的检索率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG管道仅依赖语义相似度进行检索排序，忽略了文档来源的可信度和出处信息，导致低质量或不可信来源的文档可能被优先检索，影响生成质量。

Method: 为每个文档基于其来源类型分配先验权重λ(s)，将检索得分重定义为score(q,d)=sim(q,d)×λ(s)，实现来源感知的重排序。

Result: 在120篇健康文档数据集上，来源感知重排序将Precision@5从0.48提升至0.72，并在评估的威胁模型下降低了低可信度对抗性文档的检索率。

Conclusion: 该方法在受控实验设置下展示了缓解RAG管道中来源质量退化问题的潜力，是一种简单且可解释的改进策略。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipelines rank retrieved documents by semantic similarity alone, without accounting for source provenance or credibility. This work evaluates a simple and interpretable modification to RAG retrieval ranking that incorporates domain-informed source reliability priors. Each document is assigned a prior lambda(s) based on its source type, and retrieval scores are reweighted using score(q, d) = sim(q, d) * lambda(s). The framework is evaluated against a similarity-only baseline on a 120-document health-domain corpus. In this controlled setting, source-aware reranking improves Precision@5 from 0.48 to 0.72 and reduces average adversarial document retrieval under the evaluated threat model, where low-credibility sources are identifiable via metadata. All experiments were executed on Rosie, the high-performance computing cluster at the Milwaukee School of Engineering, which provided the GPU-accelerated infrastructure necessary to run the full experimental pipeline reliably and reproducibly. These results suggest a potential mitigation strategy for source quality degradation in RAG pipelines, within the limits of the experimental setup described.

</details>


### [208] [The Scaffold Effect in Coding Agents: Harness Choice as a Hidden Variable in Coding-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22585)
*Naman Vats,Oleg Golev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 编码智能体的评测不能仅看模型名称和通过率，测试框架（harness）的选择会导致每解决一个任务的token消耗差异高达40倍，而模型间的通过率差异仅在0-8个百分点之间。框架层面的偏误是模型无关的，因此需要将harness-模型对作为评测基本单元，并报告token消耗、延迟等实际成本指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体的公开排行榜通常仅按模型名称和通过率排序，但支撑评测的harness（脚手架）往往未充分说明。当harness变化时，性能和效率的差异会混淆模型与脚手架各自的影响，导致不公平或误导性的比较。

Method: 在Terminal-Bench Pro的50个分层子集上，使用Qwen 3.6 Plus和MiniMax M2.5两种模型，跨三个开源harness（Goose、OpenCode、OpenHands-SDK）进行评估，对比通过率与token消耗等效率指标。

Result: Harness选择导致每解决一个任务的token消耗差异高达40倍；同一模型在不同harness下的通过率差异为0-8个百分点（95% Bootstrap置信区间包含零，除最大差距外）。失败模式指纹（如REASON、VERIFY/MAX_TURNS、idle-loop/TIME）在不同模型间可复现，表明存在模型无关的harness层偏误。

Conclusion: 模型名称单独作为比较单元是不完整的，应使用harness-模型对作为评测基本单元；建议在token/延迟预算下按通过率选择harness-模型对，并报告token消耗、延迟及完整harness规格。无操作回合是一种按任务等待税（wait tax），而非仅仅是token税。

Abstract: Public leaderboards for coding agents typically rank systems by model name and pass rate, while the surrounding harness (the scaffold that issues tools, manages context, and decides when to stop) is often under-specified. Model-to-model comparison is valid when the harness is fixed; when it varies, performance and efficiency conflate model and scaffold effects. We evaluate Qwen 3.6 Plus and MiniMax M2.5 across three open-source harnesses (Goose, OpenCode, OpenHands-SDK) on a stratified 50-task subset of Terminal-Bench Pro. Harness choice induces up to a 40x difference in tokens per solved task, while paired within-model pass-rate differences remain 0-8 percentage points (95% paired-task bootstrap CIs include zero except for the largest gap). Failure fingerprints replicate across models (REASON for Goose, VERIFY/MAX_TURNS for OpenHands-SDK, idle-loop/TIME for OpenCode), indicating harness-level biases that are largely model-independent. For human-centered coding-agent evaluation, model name alone is an incomplete comparison unit: harness-model pairs determine real-world cost, latency, and oversight burden; no-action turns are a per-task wait tax, not just a token tax. We therefore recommend selecting harness-model pairs by pass rate under token/latency budgets, and reporting token usage, latency, and full harness specifications alongside any model comparison. We release anonymized configs, raw trial logs, aggregated snapshots, and analysis scripts.

</details>


### [209] [MM-ShiftKV: Decode-Aware Prefill-Stage KV Selection for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22586)
*Jinsong Shu,Chenyang Wu,Zhongle Xie,Baokun Wang,Lidan Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-ShiftKV，一种针对多模态大语言模型的无训练、解码感知的KV缓存选择方法。通过构造方差扩展的查询代理来模拟解码阶段的查询行为，在预填充阶段估计KV重要性，解决了传统方法在严格缓存预算下因忽略解码阶段查询方差增大而导致的视觉token重要性误判问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型推理中KV缓存内存占用随上下文长度线性增长，视觉token数量庞大成为瓶颈。现有预填充阶段KV选择方法假设预填充时的查询能代表解码阶段的查询，但在多模态推理中该假设不成立——解码阶段查询方差远大于预填充阶段，导致在严格缓存预算下KV重要性估计不稳定，微小排序错误会丢弃语义关键的视觉token，损害定位和推理性能。

Method: MM-ShiftKV是一种无训练、解码感知的纯预填充KV选择方法。通过在预填充阶段构造方差扩展的查询代理（variance-expanded query proxies）来近似解码阶段的查询行为，然后基于这些查询代理的聚合注意力质量估计提示词中KV的重要性，从而实现更准确的视觉token选择。

Result: 在多模态基准测试上，MM-ShiftKV在严格的KV缓存预算下始终优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MM-ShiftKV通过解码感知的预填充KV选择有效解决了多模态推理中缓存预算紧张时的视觉token重要性估计问题，验证了考虑解码阶段查询方差变化对提升KV缓存效率的重要性。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) caching is essential for efficient inference in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet its memory footprint grows linearly with context length and becomes a major bottleneck due to the large number of visual tokens. Recent prefill-stage KV selection methods estimate KV importance from prefilling statistics, implicitly assuming that prefilling-time queries are representative of those encountered during decoding. We show that this assumption breaks down in multimodal inference, where decoding-time queries exhibit substantially larger variance than prefilling-stage representations, leading to unstable KV importance estimation under tight cache budgets. As a result, small ranking errors can disproportionately discard semantically critical visual tokens and degrade grounding and reasoning performance. We propose MM-ShiftKV, a training-free, decode-aware and strictly prefill-only KV selection method. MM-ShiftKV approximates decoding-time query behavior during prefilling by constructing variance-expanded query proxies and estimates prompt KV importance based on their aggregated attention mass. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that MM-ShiftKV consistently outperforms existing methods under strict KV-cache budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjuDBxAI/MM-ShiftKV.

</details>


### [210] [TriSP: Tri-Signal Structured Pruning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22587)
*Manel Kara laoua,Soumia Bouyahiaoui,Aicha Boutorh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TriSP结构化剪枝方法，结合权重幅度、激活范数和梯度敏感性进行重要性评估，并配合自适应层预算分配和LoRA恢复，在LLaMA-7B上以20%剪枝率达到6.80困惑度，50%剪枝率提升82%推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法要么依赖内存开销大的梯度估计，要么使用激活统计代理无法直接度量移除对损失的影响；此外，重要性标准与剪枝后恢复策略之间的交互也未得到系统研究。

Method: 提出TriSP重要性度量，通过几何平均融合权重幅度（按激活范数缩放）和一阶梯度敏感性，生成通道级分数；结合自适应逐层预算分配和LoRA微调恢复。

Result: 在所有测试配置下达到最低困惑度和最高零样本准确率；LLaMA-7B在20%剪枝率下WikiText-2困惑度为6.80；50%剪枝率下推理吞吐量提升82%且保持竞争力。

Conclusion: TriSP通过融合多种信号的结构化剪枝方法，在性能和效率之间取得了优越的平衡，验证了重要性准则与恢复策略协同设计的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse tasks but their deployment is constrained by the memory and compute cost of their parameters. Structured pruning addresses this by removing entire structures such as attention heads and Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) neurons to produce smaller dense models that run efficiently on standard hardware. However, existing methods rely on either gradient-based importance estimation, which is memory-prohibitive, or activation-based statistical proxies, which do not directly measure the effect of removal on the loss. Furthermore, the interaction between the importance criterion and the post-pruning recovery strategy has not been systematically studied. We propose TriSP (Tri-Signal Structured Pruning), an importance metric that combines weight magnitude scaled by activation norm with first-order gradient sensitivity via a geometric mean, producing a channel-level score that captures both structural and loss-sensitivity signals. Combined with adaptive per-layer budget allocation and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) recovery, TriSP achieves the lowest perplexity and highest zero-shot accuracy across all tested configurations, reaching 6.80 WikiText-2 perplexity at 20% pruning on LLaMA-7B. Inference throughput improves by 82% at 50% pruning, while still maintaining competitive performance.

</details>


### [211] [ParBench: A Benchmark for Reliable Evaluation of LLM Parallel Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22588)
*Samyak Jhaveri,Erel Kaplan,Tom Yotam,Le Chen,Tomer Bitan,Niranjan Hasabnis,Gal Oren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ParBench是一个面向LLM的并行API翻译评估基准框架，通过固定构建/运行/验证基础设施、仅保留内核翻译任务，并引入AST驱动的源增强来测试翻译鲁棒性。评估显示现有LLM在并行代码翻译中存在方向不对称、多文件协调、API适配不完全及对源扰动鲁棒性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算密集型软件需要在CUDA、OpenMP、OpenCL等多种并行API间迁移，LLM和自主编码代理被广泛用于此类任务，但缺乏可靠的评估方法来衡量它们是否保留了底层的并行语义（如线程索引、同步、内存管理、主机-设备协调等），导致翻译的行为有效性无法保证。

Method: ParBench采用以计算核为中心的基准框架：通过声明式基准规范固定构建、运行和验证基础设施，仅让模型翻译计算核；从多个开源HPC基准套件中提取翻译方向（CUDA、OpenMP、OpenCL、OpenMP target offload之间的交叉翻译）；引入AST驱动的、意图保持的、基线验证的源增强技术，以区分稳健翻译与表面形式的记忆。

Result: 对现有开源和商业LLM的评估揭示了并行代码翻译中持续存在的障碍：方向不对称（不同API对之间翻译难度不均）、多文件协调困难、API适配不完全、以及对源级扰动的鲁棒性不一致。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在可靠并行代码翻译方面仍存在根本性不足，ParBench为这一方向提供了可执行、可复现的评估基础设施。

Abstract: Modern compute-intensive software must migrate across a changing ecosystem of accelerators, programming APIs, compiler stacks, and portability layers, including CUDA, OpenMP, OpenCL, and OpenMP target offload. Large language models and autonomous coding agents are increasingly proposed for such migration, but the field lacks reliable ways to measure whether they preserve the low-level parallel semantics that make translations behaviorally valid, including thread indexing, synchronization, memory management, host-device coordination, and API-specific execution structure.
  We present ParBench, a kernel-centric benchmark framework for evaluating LLM-based parallel API translation under executable, reproducible conditions. ParBench fixes the surrounding build, run, and verification infrastructure through declarative benchmark specifications and asks models to translate only the computational kernels. It draws on multiple open-source HPC suites and covers representative cross-API translation directions among CUDA, OpenMP, OpenCL, and OpenMP target offload. To test whether success reflects robust translation rather than surface-form memorization, ParBench includes AST-driven, intended behavior-preserving, baseline-validated source augmentation. Evaluations on state-of-the-art open and proprietary LLMs show persistent barriers to reliable parallel code translation, including direction asymmetry, multi-file coordination, incomplete API adaptation, and uneven robustness to source-level perturbations. Code is available at https://github.com/Scientific-Computing-Lab/ParBench.

</details>


### [212] [Lexical discovery in unknown environments orchestrated by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22591)
*Rafael Sendra-Arranz,Iñaki Dellibarda Varela,Eduardo Rocon,Álvaro Gutiérrez,Manuel Cebrian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号词汇发现（NSLD）框架，让LLM智能体群体通过指称游戏，在未知环境中自主组织共享的外星词汇，将发现的新词锚定到自然语言语义空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在未知环境（如行星或深海探测）中需要为新实体共享词汇，而这些实体在人类语言中尚无名称，现有方法无法支持自主词汇发明与共识达成。

Method: NSLD框架：每个智能体结合冻结的CLIP视觉编码器、私有FAISS向量索引和纯文本LLM，通过指称游戏对分布外视觉指代物进行协商，基于嵌入空间语义邻近性将发现的外星词汇锚定到自然语言。

Result: 在最多20个智能体和10个视觉指代物的模拟中达成共识，并通过三个分析模型（R²>0.95）表征了收敛动力学。

Conclusion: NSLD框架为自主探索任务中的预部署规划提供了第一步，使智能体群体能够自主生成感知锚定的共享词汇，扩展人类词汇表。

Abstract: Populations of autonomous agents deployed in unknown environments (e.g. planetary or deep-sea exploration) must develop shared vocabularies to refer to entities that have no name in any human language. We propose the Neuro-Symbolic Lexical Discovery (NSLD) framework, in which a population of LLM-based agents plays a referential game over out-of-distribution visual referents, autonomously self-organising a shared alien lexicon. Each agent combines a frozen CLIP vision encoder with a private FAISS vector index and a text-only LLM. Crucially, discovered alien words are anchored to natural language via semantic proximity in the embedding space, enlarging the human vocabulary with new perceptually grounded words. Consensus is reached in simulations with populations of up to twenty agents and ten visual referents. Convergence dynamics are characterised through three analytical models achieving R^2 > 0.95, representing a first step towards pre-deployment planning in autonomous exploration missions.

</details>


### [213] [Structure Over Scale: Schema-Constrained Causal Graphs for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22592)
*Marc Saouda,Rajprakash Bale,Eren Aldis,Cloves Almeida*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCG-RAG 提出用模式约束的因果图替代传统实体-关系图，将语料库蒸馏为固定类型化因果变量的紧凑两层级图，在保持答案质量的同时将图节点减少3-20倍、构建LLM调用减少8-135倍，并实现领域专家可审计、可纠正、可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG系统对实体和关系进行穷举式抽取，导致图的规模和构建成本随语料长度扩展，而非随查询所需的推理需求扩展，造成计算资源浪费且难以审计。

Method: 提出HCG-RAG框架，包含一个自动化pipeline：将语料库蒸馏为固定、类型化的因果变量词汇表，并基于此构建紧凑的两层级因果图。使用模式约束替代开放式抽取，生成层次化因果结构。

Result: 相比最耗费LLM的基线MS-GraphRAG，节点数减少3-20倍，构建时LLM调用减少8-135倍；因果图作为结构化检索过滤器贡献+6个百分点的提升；在医学和临床基准上匹配或超越最佳实体-关系系统。

Conclusion: 在具有可发现层次化因果结构的所有领域中，只有施加更高层次组织的方法才能超越平面实体-关系检索，说明图中放置什么内容比包含多少节点更重要。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) grounds answers in structured knowledge, but current systems extract entities and relationships exhaustively, producing graphs whose size and construction cost scale with corpus length rather than with the reasoning a query requires. We introduce HCG-RAG (Hierarchical Causal Graph RAG), which replaces open-ended extraction with schema-constrained causal graphs: an automated pipeline distills a corpus into a fixed, typed vocabulary of causal variables and materializes a compact two-tier graph over it. Our schema-constrained graphs match entity-relation baselines on answer quality at a fraction of the cost: 3-20x fewer nodes, 8x-135x fewer build-time LLM calls than the most LLM-intensive baseline (MS-GraphRAG), and graphs compact enough for a domain expert to audit, correct, and extend. On medical and clinical benchmarks, including a neurologist-validated epilepsy dataset, HCG-RAG matches or exceeds the best entity-relation systems.
